- **transcription-automation**: Automate audio/video transcription, meeting notes, subtitle generation, and content processing ([AgnesAI-Labs/agnet-skills-cn](https://github.com/AgnesAI-Labs/agnet-skills-cn/tree/HEAD/transcription-automation) · ★1)
- **transcription-check**: Shadow audit of your app's stored speech-to-text transcripts. Cuts audio clips with ffmpeg, re-transcribes them with Whisper as the "ears", and has Claude judge stored text vs what was actually said (wrong language, translation, invented content, accuracy), then reports agreement with your production verifier. Trigger - "/transcription-check [YYYY-MM-DD]", a scheduled transcription-check routine, or "run the transcription check". ([Shadysmetools/claude-skills](https://github.com/Shadysmetools/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/transcription-check))
- **transcription-cli**: Command-line WhisperX transcription workflows, safe command construction, and CLI troubleshooting. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/whisperx/sub-skills/transcription-cli) · ★357 · 2 copies)
- **transcription-corrector**: 转录稿纠错与轻度优化。本技能应在用户需要按用户词典纠正 ASR 转录稿同音字与英文专有名称漂移时使用。不要用于：重写为课程章节、报告、总结，或完全空白的素材创作。 ([cat-xierluo/legal-skills](https://github.com/cat-xierluo/legal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/transcription-corrector) · ★820)
- **transcription-deapi**: Transcribe audio and video from URLs using Whisper AI via deAPI. Supports YouTube, X, Twitch, Kick, and media files (mp3, wav, m4a, flac, ogg, mp4, webm, etc) ([tecnosonda/skills](https://github.com/tecnosonda/skills/tree/HEAD/transcription-deapi))
- **transcription-error-voip-call-dropouts-013146152617f9b2**: Flag the gap ranges in the transcript as audio missing, ask the rep for the missing points in the follow-up, and check the VoIP recording path (dual recording or provider side capture) if dropouts repeat, because network dropouts removed whole sentences and the summarizer bridged the holes with plausible filler. Use when you see this exact failure: transcription error VoIP call dropouts create gaps in summary failure. Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/transcription-error-voip-call-dropouts-013146152617f9b2))
- **transcription-expert**: Analyzes meeting transcriptions, interviews, and documentation to extract business insights, domain knowledge, action items, decisions, and key concepts. Use when processing transcriptions, extracting knowledge from discussions, or analyzing recorded conversations. ([ranjanpoudel1234/ai-tools](https://github.com/ranjanpoudel1234/ai-tools/tree/HEAD/Opencode/skills/transcription-expert) · ★1)
- **transcription-expert**: Expert AI transcription and multimedia content processing for Vietnamese and English audio/video. Use when the user needs to transcribe audio or video to text, perform speaker diarization, clean up transcripts, generate subtitles/SRT files, summarize spoken content, or handle transcription tasks involving marketing, technology, or business terminology. Triggers on requests for audio transcription, video-to-text, subtitle generation, speaker identification, transcript cleaning/summarization, or processing any multimedia content into written form. ([lilbom32/tay-tang](https://github.com/lilbom32/tay-tang/tree/HEAD/skills))
- **transcription-factor-activity-inference**: Use when you have a time-series expression matrix plus TF–TG interaction data and need to identify which TFs significantly regulate each subpath of a tree-structure model and when they are activated or repressed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-activity-inference))
- **transcription-factor-activity-inference**: Use when you need to score TF activities from RNA perturbation compendia (e.g. Holland et al. 2020 transcriptomics data) using a prior-knowledge gene regulatory network like DoRothEA and rank inference methods by sensitivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-activity-inference))
- **transcription-factor-activity-inference-from-autoencoder-latent-space**: Use when a counts table with pre-defined cell/spot clusters (e.g., SIMLR annotations over a Visium breast cancer section) must be linked to specific transcription factors via an autoencoder latent space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-activity-inference-from-autoencoder-latent-space))
- **transcription-factor-activity-inference-from-target-genes**: Use when a multi-omics case-vs-control study (e.g., metformin-treated MCF7 transcriptomics) needs TF activity inferred from target-gene expression signatures as Step 1 of a SignalingProfiler 2.0 pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/transcription-factor-activity-inference-from-target-genes))
- **transcription-factor-activity-inference-linear-model**: Use when you have preprocessed single-cell RNA-seq (Scanpy/scVelo adata) plus a TF binding-site matrix and want to estimate time- and fate-specific TF activities along stochastic cell-fate differentiation trajectories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-activity-inference-linear-model))
- **transcription-factor-activity-projection**: Use when you have a pre-processed single-cell count matrix (e.g. PBMC SingleCellExperiment) plus a TF-target prior such as DoRothEA, and want cell-level TF regulon activity scores and projections. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-activity-projection))
- **transcription-factor-affinity-feature-engineering**: Use when raw DNA binding sequences from uPBM, HT-SELEX or gcPBM must be converted into structural/mechanical feature matrices to train or apply a transcription-factor affinity regressor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-affinity-feature-engineering))
- **transcription-factor-affinity-regression**: Use when experimental TF-DNA binding data (uPBM training files, gcPBM concentration files, HT-SELEX per-cycle count files) must be converted into per-sequence affinity predictions with an R2-validated random-forest regressor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-affinity-regression))
- **transcription-factor-binding-depletion-quantification**: Use when after bias-correcting ATAC-seq cutsite signal (using ATACorrect or equivalent) when you have a set of genomic regions of interest (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-binding-depletion-quantification))
- **transcription-factor-binding-motif-enrichment**: Use when converting a co-expression network (GCN) to a directed GRN and candidate TF→TG edges (e.g. from GRNBoost2/GENIE3-style regression) must be filtered by cis-regulatory motif enrichment to keep only motif-supported regulators. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-binding-motif-enrichment))
- **transcription-factor-binding-motif-refinement**: Use when an initial MI-based GRN (e.g., from RNA-Seq time series) should be filtered or upgraded with motif evidence from upstream promoter sequences, or when reproducing Augusta's 'with refinement' benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-binding-motif-refinement))
- **transcription-factor-binding-motif-scoring**: Use when you have anchor-centered sequences (FASTA with per-sequence scores in headers) and a motif PWM, and need motif match positions/scores relative to the anchor for positional enrichment profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-binding-motif-scoring))
- **transcription-factor-binding-profile-interpretation**: Use when a non-coding SNP needs functional interpretation against a transcription factor binding profile, and you want saturated mutagenesis SAD heat maps that reveal which motif and which allele-specific substitutions drive predicted binding activity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-binding-profile-interpretation))
- **transcription-factor-binding-site-detection**: Use when converting enhancer FASTA sequences (e.g. data/train/NHLF.fasta) into per-motif TFBS count/frequency features for a deep learning enhancer predictor, using HOCOMOCO v11 PWMs with p-value thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-binding-site-detection))
- **transcription-factor-binding-site-mapping**: Use when you have a promoter nucleotide sequence (FASTA) and a known TF consensus/position-specific motif, and need to enumerate motif matches and test positional overlap with other factors' binding sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-binding-site-mapping))
- **transcription-factor-binding-site-matrix-construction**: Use when preparing FateCompass inputs from single-cell transcriptomics (e.g., a pre-processed AnnData from Dyngen simulation or pancreatic endocrine differentiation) and you must supply the TF/motif-vs-expressed-gene binding-site matrix needed for TF activity inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-binding-site-matrix-construction))
- **transcription-factor-binding-site-proximity-annotation**: Use when ChIP-seq peak coordinate files (e.g., MACS2 peak lists from GSE136050 H-NS and GSE136191 LuxR datasets) must be related by interval distance — e.g., counting how many of a structural peak class (H-NS doublets) lie within 1 kb of a TF peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-binding-site-proximity-annotation))
- **transcription-factor-candidate-selection**: Use when a cell type has already been identified in a scRNA-Seq dataset (SINCERA S4 object) and you need to find its driving forces — key transcription factors — from differential expression and network centrality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-candidate-selection))
- **transcription-factor-cluster-activity-analysis**: Use when a time-course single-cell dataset needs TF activity profiles aligned to ligand-receptor dynamics — e.g. to test whether co-varying LR pairs are causally linked to temporal TF clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-cluster-activity-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-co-occupancy-intersection**: Use when two transcription factors have ChIP-seq peak sets and transcriptomic differential-expression data, and the goal is promoters and downstream genes co-occupied and co-regulated by both factors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-co-occupancy-intersection))
- **transcription-factor-cofactor-motif-annotation**: Use when you have DESSO motifs/TFBSs and ENCODE ChIP-seq peaks for multiple TFs in one cell line and need to classify indirect binding and infer TF-cofactor tethering from motif co-occurrence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-cofactor-motif-annotation))
- **transcription-factor-composite-ranking**: Use when DC3 has assigned scRNA-seq and scATAC-seq cells to subpopulations and you need to rank transcription factors driving each subpopulation by joint expression and motif enrichment evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-composite-ranking))
- **transcription-factor-cooperative-binding-detection**: Use when you have a chromVARDeviations object with precomputed bias-corrected deviations and z-scores for multiple annotation sets (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-cooperative-binding-detection))
- **transcription-factor-database-selection**: Use when running TFvelo on scRNA-seq data and you need to restrict the TF pool per target gene — e.g., preparing the gene regulatory input before weight initialization and velocity inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-database-selection))
- **transcription-factor-enrichment**: Find transcription factors (TFs) that significantly regulate a given gene list. Uses enrichment analysis against curated TF-target databases (ENCODE ChEA3 API, DoRothEA gene sets built-in) and returns ranked TFs with enrichment statistics and network visualization. ([MDhewei/bioinfor-claw](https://github.com/MDhewei/bioinfor-claw/tree/HEAD/gene-list-analysis/transcription-factor-enrichment) · ★65)
- **transcription-factor-footprint-scoring**: Use when after bias-corrected ATAC-seq signal tracks (bigWig files) have been generated and you need to quantify transcription factor binding strength within open chromatin regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-footprint-scoring))
- **transcription-factor-hit-prioritization**: Use when you have a fitted PRESCIENT model (data_pt) on time-series scRNA-seq and need to identify which transcription factors significantly bias differentiation toward a target fate before designing an in vitro perturbational screen. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-hit-prioritization))
- **transcription-factor-marker-prioritization**: Use when scRNAseq clusters have been defined (e.g., via rCASC clustering) and the goal is to identify transcription factors whose target-derived latent representation drives cluster aggregation, rather than generic differential expression markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-marker-prioritization))
- **transcription-factor-motif-database-matching**: Use when a set of de novo TF sequence motifs (e.g., predicted by DESSO from ENCODE ChIP-seq peaks) needs to be labeled as matching documented JASPAR/TRANSFAC motifs or flagged as novel/undocumented. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-database-matching))
- **transcription-factor-motif-enrichment**: Use when a Mowgli factor's H(atac) matrix rows must be biologically annotated: top-weighted peaks per factor are tested for TF motif over-representation to name the factor's putative regulator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-enrichment))
- **transcription-factor-motif-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have binarized topic-enriched regions from a cisTopic model on single-cell ATAC-seq data and want to recover topic-specific TF cistromes and master regulators such as SPI1 and CEBPB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-enrichment-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-motif-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when assigning functional titles or annotations to BloodGen3 (or similar fixed) module gene lists, and transcription-factor motif enrichment (e.g. RcisTarget) is needed as convergent evidence alongside ontology/pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-enrichment-interpretation))
- **transcription-factor-motif-genomic-coordinate-annotation**: Use when you have ATAC-seq BAM files and want to detect transcription factor footprints—characteristic depletion patterns of Tn5 insertions around protein-bound motif sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-motif-genomic-coordinate-annotation))
- **transcription-factor-motif-matching**: Use when you have corrected ATAC-seq footprint scores (from ATACorrect and ScoreBigwig) at open chromatin regions and a motif database (e.g., JASPAR PWMs), and you need to determine which TF motif matches are actually occupied across one or more experimental conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-motif-matching))
- **transcription-factor-motif-occurrence-analysis**: Use when the input is a genome-wide TF motif occurrence track (e.g. JASPAR PWM-derived BED with strand annotations) and the goal is to quantify orientation bias — same vs opposite strand — between consecutive or paired motif occurrences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-occurrence-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-motif-scanning**: Use when you have FASTA sequences under predicted ChIP-enriched peaks (plus matched control sequences) and need to verify enrichment of an expected TF binding motif family, or compare motif enrichment across peak ranks or platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-scanning))
- **transcription-factor-network-analysis**: Use when a transcriptomics (or phospho-proteomic) dataset must be summarized into per-regulator activity scores and the user runs multiple inference methods simultaneously and needs one consensus estimate per regulator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-network-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-network-inference**: Use when a CellexalVR in-session cell group needs a TF-only co-expression network: the input is expression values for the selected cells and the goal is a tf_network.txt consumed by the VR TF network generator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-network-inference))
- **transcription-factor-panel-verification**: Use when a TF-based SCA latent-space frequency matrix plus cluster assignments (e.g., setA clusters 1 and 2) are available and the goal is to extract and verify an optimal gene marker panel (≤4 genes, negations allowed) per cluster with COMET. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-panel-verification))
